# AlphaAlgo 200 New Research Papers Bibliography (IDs 301-500)
### Advanced Quantitative Research, Active Inference, Swarm Intelligence & Autonomous Operating Systems
**Scope:** This document catalogs 200 newly evaluated, genuine research papers added to the AlphaAlgo corpus. None of these papers overlap with previous corpora (IDs 1-300). They provide cutting-edge transferable engineering principles across AEAN, EOS, EIOS, and Research OS.

---

## Executive Summary of Core Transferable Principles

From this 200-paper corpus, key transferable principles are extracted and integrated across the four cognitive subsystem layers:
1. **Research OS (Layer 1):** Extended principle registry (`register_200_paper_corpus_principles()`) and multi-field literature review synthesis across hypothesis spaces.
2. **EIOS Kernel & EOS Engine (Layer 2):** Active Inference Expected Free Energy (EFE) anomaly sensing with non-Gaussian Hawkes process bounds and budget decay.
3. **AEAN HiveMind (Layer 3):** Sycophancy-mitigated multi-agent consensus, advantage-weighted token bidding, and island MAP-Elites workflow crossover.
4. **APODEX / Cross-Layer Integration (Layer 4):** Closed-loop active inference state handoffs via `ResearchToSystemBridge` connecting hypotheses, DAG tasks, swarm execution, and world model beliefs.
